# Repository folder structure

Snapshot: 2026-09-29T13:57:56.042863+00:00 (UTC). Paths are relative to the repository root.

This is a **folder-only** inventory, not a list of files. Every discovered project folder is listed below. Git internals, installed dependencies, and tool/bytecode caches are explicitly collapsed. Directory symlinks are shown but not followed, so external datasets are not recursively inventoried. No file contents were read to build this inventory. Active runs may create additional folders after this snapshot.

## Main locations

| Folder | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `src/` | Implementation and analysis modules. |
| `configs/` | Dataset and experiment configurations. |
| `scripts/` | Execution and utility scripts. |
| `tests/` | Tests and small fixtures. |
| `docs/` | Plans, methodology, policies, and handoffs. |
| `runs/` | Versioned experiment outputs and continuation state. |
| `artifacts/` | Reports, audit packages, figures, and other generated evidence. |
| `data/` | Data locations; preserve raw contents. |
| `experiments/ and archive/` | Historical experiment material; preserve existing outputs. |
| `presentation_assets/ and tutorials/` | Presentation material and tutorials. |
| `scratch/` | Working material; directory name alone does not imply permission to delete. |
| `ma_thesis_env/` | Repository Python environment; dependencies omitted from the tree. |
